- **diagnosing-bugs**: Phase 1 & 4 系統化 6 階段除錯迴圈與 Bug 根因追蹤技能（整合 diagnosing-bugs、bug-tracker 嚴重度分級與 quick-fix-patterns 常見錯誤速查）。 觸發詞：/diagnosing-bugs, /fix, diagnose, debug this, bug-tracker, quick-fix, 除錯, 抓蟲, 效能退化. - **diagnosing-bugs**: 面向棘手缺陷和性能回退的诊断循环。适用于用户说 “diagnose” / “debug this”，或报告某些东西 broken、throwing、failing、slow 时。 ([shreyvijayvargiya/cizle](https://github.com/shreyvijayvargiya/cizle/tree/HEAD/library/v4/skills/mattpocock-skills-zh-cn-diagnosing-bugs-82551a) · ★2 · 4.5k installs · 7 copies)

- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnosis loop for unclear, hard, flaky, or performance-sensitive bugs. ([tsai09495/matt-pocock-engineering](https://github.com/tsai09495/matt-pocock-engineering/tree/HEAD/plugins/matt-engineering/skills/diagnosing-bugs) · ★2)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: 针对棘手缺陷和性能回退的诊断循环。用户提出“诊断”/“调试这个问题”，或报告某项功能损坏、抛出异常、失败或运行缓慢时使用。 ([15102045545/skills-cn](https://github.com/15102045545/skills-cn/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/diagnosing-bugs) · ★1)
- **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnostic techniques for hard bugs and performance regressions — reproduce and minimise first, falsifiable predictions, single-variable probes, regression-before-fix with a correct seam, and cleanup. Use when something is broken, throwing, failing, or slow and the cause is not obvious. Role-neutral and activity-scoped: the technique confers no authority, performs no routing, and never decides which role performs which activity. - **diagnosing-bugs**: Diagnose non-trivial bugs, flakes, hangs, and performance regressions.
